RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = CS12_A_1_5
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024

RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = CS12_A_2_7
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Vineyard Features: Vitis vinifera cultivars Cabernet Sauvignon (clone FPS 8 grafted on 5C rootstock and planted in 1997) and Pinot noir (clone FPS 23 grafted on rootstock Freedom and planted in 2001) were used in this study. Both varieties were grown nearby Modesto (USA, CA) in sandy clay loam in an east-west orientation with 10 foot row spacing and 5 foot vine spacing.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = CS12_A_1_10
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

Both varieties were grown nearby Modesto (USA, CA) in sandy clay loam in an east-west orientation with 10 foot row spacing and 5 foot vine spacing.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = CS13_A_2_4
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = CS13_A_1_12
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.

Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).

!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = CS14_A_2_10
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment (cluster thinning): unthinned
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cultivar: Pinot noir
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Vineyard Features: Vitis vinifera cultivars Cabernet Sauvignon (clone FPS 8 grafted on 5C rootstock and planted in 1997) and Pinot noir (clone FPS 23 grafted on rootstock Freedom and planted in 2001) were used in this study. Both varieties were grown nearby Modesto (USA, CA) in sandy clay loam in an east-west orientation with 10 foot row spacing and 5 foot vine spacing.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = PN12_A_2_2
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).

!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters

!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = sugar content (g/100ml): 3.11
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment (cluster thinning): unthinned
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cultivar: Pinot noir
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Vineyard Features: Vitis vinifera cultivars Cabernet Sauvignon (clone FPS 8 grafted on 5C rootstock and planted in 1997) and Pinot noir (clone FPS 23 grafted on rootstock Freedom and planted in 2001) were used in this study. Both varieties were grown nearby Modesto (USA, CA) in sandy clay loam in an east-west orientation with 10 foot row spacing and 5 foot vine spacing.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = PN13_A_2_2
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day after veraison: 28
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = sugar content (g/100ml): 19.58
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment (cluster thinning): unthinned
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cultivar: Pinot noir
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Vineyard Features: Vitis vinifera cultivars Cabernet Sauvignon (clone FPS 8 grafted on 5C rootstock and planted in 1997) and Pinot noir (clone FPS 23 grafted on rootstock Freedom and planted in 2001) were used in this study. Both varieties were grown nearby Modesto (USA, CA) in sandy clay loam in an east-west orientation with 10 foot row spacing and 5 foot vine spacing.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = PN13_A_2_7
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment (cluster thinning): unthinned
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cultivar: Pinot noir
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Vineyard Features: Vitis vinifera cultivars Cabernet Sauvignon (clone FPS 8 grafted on 5C rootstock and planted in 1997) and Pinot noir (clone FPS 23 grafted on rootstock Freedom and planted in 2001) were used in this study. Both varieties were grown nearby Modesto (USA, CA) in sandy clay loam in an east-west orientation with 10 foot row spacing and 5 foot vine spacing.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = PN14_A_2_6
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = year: 2014
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = time point: 11
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = time point replicate: 2
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day after veraison: 52
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = sugar content (g/100ml): 24.1
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment (cluster thinning): unthinned
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cultivar: Pinot noir
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Vineyard Features: Vitis vinifera cultivars Cabernet Sauvignon (clone FPS 8 grafted on 5C rootstock and planted in 1997) and Pinot noir (clone FPS 23 grafted on rootstock Freedom and planted in 2001) were used in this study. Both varieties were grown nearby Modesto (USA, CA) in sandy clay loam in an east-west orientation with 10 foot row spacing and 5 foot vine spacing.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was isolated using the Spectrum Plant Total RNA kit (Sigma-Aldrich) following the manufacturer’s instructions with some modifications (Fasoli et al., 2012). RNA quality and quantity were determined using a Nanodrop 2000 spectrophotometer (Thermo Scientific) and a Bioanalyzer Chip RNA 7500 series II (Agilent).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 220 non-directional cDNA libraries were prepared from 2.5 μg of total RNA using the Illumina TruSeq RNA Sample preparation protocol (Illumina Inc., San Diego, CA, USA). Library quality was determined using the Agilent High Sensitivity DNA kit on the Agilent 2100 bioanalyzer, and the quantity was determined by quantitative PCR using the KAPA Library Quantification kit (KapaBiosystems).
!Sample_description = PN14_A_2_11
!Sample_data_processing = The reads were aligned onto the grapevine 12x reference genome PN40024 using TopHat v2.0.6 with default parameters
!Sample_data_processing = Mapped reads were used to reconstruct the transcripts using Cufflinks v2.0.2 and the reference genome annotation V1
!Sample_data_processing = The normalized expression of each transcript was calculated as RPKM (Reads Per Kilobase per Million mapped reads) for each sample.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: 12X reference genome PN400024
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: tab delimited text file containing the normalized reads counts RPKM  for all the samples.
